```
    90→// commitWithMetadata commits the session changes with metadata.
    91→func commitWithMetadata() error { //nolint:maintidx // already present in codebase
    92→	// Get the agent for hook input parsing and session ID transformation
    93→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
    94→	if err != nil {
    95→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
    96→	}
    97→
    98→	// Parse hook input using agent interface
    99→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookStop, os.Stdin)
   100→	if err != nil {
   101→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   102→	}
   103→
   104→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   105→	logging.Info(logCtx, "stop",
   106→		slog.String("hook", "stop"),
   107→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
   108→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   109→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
   110→	)
   111→
   112→	sessionID := input.SessionID
   113→	if sessionID == "" {
   114→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
   115→	}
   116→
   117→	transcriptPath := input.SessionRef
   118→	if transcriptPath == "" || !fileExists(transcriptPath) {
   119→		return fmt.Errorf("transcript file not found or empty: %s", transcriptPath)
   120→	}
   121→
   122→	// Create session metadata folder using the entire session ID (preserves original date on resume)
   123→	// Use AbsPath to ensure we create at repo root, not relative to cwd
   124→	sessionDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(sessionID)
   125→	sessionDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(sessionDir)
   126→	if err != nil {
   127→		sessionDirAbs = sessionDir // Fallback to relative
   128→	}
   129→	if err := os.MkdirAll(sessionDirAbs, 0o750); err != nil {
   130→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session directory: %w", err)
   131→	}
   132→
   133→	// Wait for Claude Code to flush the transcript file.
   134→	// The stop hook fires before the transcript is fully written to disk.
   135→	// We poll for our own hook_progress sentinel entry in the file tail,
   136→	// which guarantees all prior entries have been flushed.
   137→	waitForTranscriptFlush(transcriptPath, time.Now())
   138→
   139→	// Copy transcript
   140→	logFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName)
   141→	if err := copyFile(transcriptPath, logFile); err != nil {
   142→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to copy transcript: %w", err)
   143→	}
   144→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Copied transcript to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.TranscriptFileName)
   145→
   146→	// Load pre-prompt state (captured on UserPromptSubmit)
   147→	// Needed for transcript offset and file change detection
   148→	preState, err := LoadPrePromptState(sessionID)
   149→	if err != nil {
   150→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   151→	}
   152→
   153→	// Determine transcript offset: prefer pre-prompt state, fall back to session state.
   154→	// Pre-prompt state has the offset when the transcript path was available at prompt time.
   155→	// Session state has the offset updated after each successful checkpoint save (auto-commit).
   156→	var transcriptOffset int
   157→	if preState != nil && preState.StepTranscriptStart > 0 {
   158→		transcriptOffset = preState.StepTranscriptStart
   159→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state found: parsing transcript from line %d\n", transcriptOffset)
   160→	} else {
   161→		// Fall back to session state (e.g., auto-commit strategy updates it after each save)
   162→		sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   163→		if loadErr != nil {
   164→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   165→		}
   166→		if sessionState != nil && sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart > 0 {
   167→			transcriptOffset = sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart
   168→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Session state found: parsing transcript from line %d\n", transcriptOffset)
   169→		}
   170→	}
   171→
   172→	// Parse transcript (optionally from offset for strategies that track transcript position)
   173→	// When transcriptOffset > 0, only parse NEW lines since the last checkpoint
   174→	var transcript []transcriptLine
   175→	var totalLines int
   176→	if transcriptOffset > 0 {
   177→		// Parse only NEW lines since last checkpoint
   178→		transcript, totalLines, err = parseTranscriptFromLine(transcriptPath, transcriptOffset)
   179→		if err != nil {
   180→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript from line %d: %w", transcriptOffset, err)
   181→		}
   182→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Parsed %d new transcript lines (total: %d)\n", len(transcript), totalLines)
   183→	} else {
   184→		// First prompt or no session state - parse entire transcript
   185→		// Use parseTranscriptFromLine with offset 0 to also get totalLines
   186→		transcript, totalLines, err = parseTranscriptFromLine(transcriptPath, 0)
   187→		if err != nil {
   188→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   189→		}
   190→	}
   191→
   192→	// Extract all prompts since last checkpoint for prompt file
   193→	allPrompts := extractUserPrompts(transcript)
   194→	promptFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.PromptFileName)
   195→	promptContent := strings.Join(allPrompts, "\n\n---\n\n")
   196→	if err := os.WriteFile(promptFile, []byte(promptContent), 0o600); err != nil {
   197→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write prompt file: %w", err)
   198→	}
   199→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted %d prompt(s) to: %s\n", len(allPrompts), sessionDir+"/"+paths.PromptFileName)
   200→
   201→	// Extract summary
   202→	summaryFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.SummaryFileName)
   203→	summary := extractLastAssistantMessage(transcript)
   204→	if err := os.WriteFile(summaryFile, []byte(summary), 0o600); err != nil {
   205→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write summary file: %w", err)
   206→	}
   207→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted summary to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.SummaryFileName)
   208→
   209→	// Get modified files from transcript
   210→	modifiedFiles := extractModifiedFiles(transcript)
   211→
   212→	// Generate commit message from last user prompt
   213→	lastPrompt := ""
   214→	if len(allPrompts) > 0 {
   215→		lastPrompt = allPrompts[len(allPrompts)-1]
   216→	}
   217→	commitMessage := generateCommitMessage(lastPrompt)
   218→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Using commit message: %s\n", commitMessage)
   219→
   220→	// Get repo root for path conversion (not cwd, since Claude may be in a subdirectory)
   221→	// Using cwd would filter out files in sibling directories (paths starting with ..)
   222→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   223→	if err != nil {
   224→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   225→	}
   226→
   227→	if preState != nil {
   228→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state: %d pre-existing untracked files\n", len(preState.UntrackedFiles))
   229→	}
   230→
   231→	// Compute new and deleted files (single git status call)
   232→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preState.PreUntrackedFiles())
   233→	if err != nil {
   234→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   235→	}
   236→
   237→	// Filter and normalize all paths (CLI responsibility)
   238→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   239→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   240→	if changes != nil {
   241→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   242→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   243→	}
   244→
   245→	// Check if there are any changes to commit
   246→	totalChanges := len(relModifiedFiles) + len(relNewFiles) + len(relDeletedFiles)
   247→	if totalChanges == 0 {
   248→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No files were modified during this session\n")
   249→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Skipping commit\n")
   250→		// Still transition phase even when skipping commit — the turn is ending.
   251→		transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   252→		// Clean up state even when skipping
   253→		if err := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); err != nil {
   254→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   255→		}
   256→		return nil
   257→	}
   258→
   259→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files modified during session (%d):\n", len(relModifiedFiles))
   260→	for _, file := range relModifiedFiles {
   261→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   262→	}
   263→	if len(relNewFiles) > 0 {
   264→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "New files created (%d):\n", len(relNewFiles))
   265→		for _, file := range relNewFiles {
   266→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  + %s\n", file)
   267→		}
   268→	}
   269→	if len(relDeletedFiles) > 0 {
   270→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files deleted (%d):\n", len(relDeletedFiles))
   271→		for _, file := range relDeletedFiles {
   272→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   273→		}
   274→	}
   275→
   276→	// Create context file before saving changes
   277→	contextFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.ContextFileName)
   278→	if err := createContextFileMinimal(contextFile, commitMessage, sessionID, promptFile, summaryFile, transcript); err != nil {
   279→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create context file: %w", err)
   280→	}
   281→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Created context file: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.ContextFileName)
   282→
   283→	// Get git author from local/global config
   284→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   285→	if err != nil {
   286→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   287→	}
   288→
   289→	// Get the configured strategy
   290→	strat := GetStrategy()
   291→
   292→	// Ensure strategy setup is in place (auto-installs git hook, gitignore, etc. if needed)
   293→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   294→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
   295→	}
   296→
   297→	// Get agent type from the currently executing hook agent (authoritative source)
   298→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   299→	if hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent(); agentErr == nil {
   300→		agentType = hookAgent.Type()
   301→	}
   302→
   303→	// Get transcript position from pre-prompt state (captured at step/turn start)
   304→	var transcriptIdentifierAtStart string
   305→	var transcriptLinesAtStart int
   306→	if preState != nil {
   307→		transcriptIdentifierAtStart = preState.LastTranscriptIdentifier
   308→		transcriptLinesAtStart = preState.StepTranscriptStart
   309→	}
   310→
   311→	// Calculate token usage for this checkpoint (Claude Code specific)
   312→	var tokenUsage *agent.TokenUsage
   313→	if transcriptPath != "" {
   314→		// Subagents are stored in a subagents/ directory next to the main transcript
   315→		subagentsDir := filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(transcriptPath), sessionID, "subagents")
   316→		usage, err := claudecode.CalculateTotalTokenUsage(transcriptPath, transcriptLinesAtStart, subagentsDir)
   317→		if err != nil {
   318→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to calculate token usage: %v\n", err)
   319→		} else {
   320→			tokenUsage = usage
   321→		}
   322→	}
   323→
   324→	// Build fully-populated save context and delegate to strategy
   325→	ctx := strategy.SaveContext{
   326→		SessionID:                sessionID,
   327→		ModifiedFiles:            relModifiedFiles,
   328→		NewFiles:                 relNewFiles,
   329→		DeletedFiles:             relDeletedFiles,
   330→		MetadataDir:              sessionDir,
   331→		MetadataDirAbs:           sessionDirAbs,
   332→		CommitMessage:            commitMessage,
   333→		TranscriptPath:           transcriptPath,
   334→		AuthorName:               author.Name,
   335→		AuthorEmail:              author.Email,
   336→		AgentType:                agentType,
   337→		StepTranscriptIdentifier: transcriptIdentifierAtStart,
   338→		StepTranscriptStart:      transcriptLinesAtStart,
   339→		TokenUsage:               tokenUsage,
   340→	}
   341→
   342→	if err := strat.SaveChanges(ctx); err != nil {
   343→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save changes: %w", err)
   344→	}
   345→
   346→	// Update session state with new transcript position for strategies that create
   347→	// commits on the active branch (auto-commit strategy). This prevents parsing old transcript
   348→	// lines on subsequent checkpoints.
   349→	// Note: Shadow strategy tracks transcript position per-step via StepTranscriptStart in
   350→	// pre-prompt state, but doesn't advance CheckpointTranscriptStart in session state because
   351→	// its checkpoints accumulate all files touched across the entire session.
   352→	if strat.Name() == strategy.StrategyNameAutoCommit {
   353→		// Load session state for updating transcript position
   354→		sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   355→		if loadErr != nil {
   356→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   357→		}
   358→		// Create session state lazily if it doesn't exist (backward compat for resumed sessions
   359→		// or if InitializeSession was never called/failed)
   360→		if sessionState == nil {
   361→			sessionState = &strategy.SessionState{
   362→				SessionID: sessionID,
   363→			}
   364→		}
   365→		sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = totalLines
   366→		sessionState.StepCount++
   367→		if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(sessionState); updateErr != nil {
   368→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", updateErr)
   369→		} else {
   370→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Updated session state: transcript position=%d, checkpoint=%d\n",
   371→				totalLines, sessionState.StepCount)
   372→		}
   373→	}
   374→
   375→	// Fire EventTurnEnd to transition session phase (all strategies).
   376→	// This moves ACTIVE → IDLE or ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE.
   377→	// For ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE, HandleTurnEnd dispatches ActionCondense.
   378→	transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   379→
   380→	// Clean up pre-prompt state (CLI responsibility)
   381→	if err := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); err != nil {
   382→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   383→	}
   384→
   385→	return nil
   386→}
   387→
   388→// handleClaudeCodePostTodo handles the PostToolUse[TodoWrite] hook for subagent checkpoints.
   389→// Creates a checkpoint if we're in a subagent context (active pre-task file exists).
   390→// Skips silently if not in subagent context (main agent).
   391→func handleClaudeCodePostTodo() error {
   392→	input, err := parseSubagentCheckpointHookInput(os.Stdin)
   393→	if err != nil {
   394→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PostToolUse[TodoWrite] input: %w", err)
   395→	}
   396→
   397→	// Get agent for logging context
   398→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   399→	if err != nil {
   400→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   401→	}
   402→
   403→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   404→	logging.Info(logCtx, "post-todo",
   405→		slog.String("hook", "post-todo"),
   406→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
   407→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   408→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.TranscriptPath),
   409→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
   410→	)
   411→
   412→	// Check if we're in a subagent context by looking for an active pre-task file
   413→	taskToolUseID, found := FindActivePreTaskFile()
   414→	if !found {
   415→		// Not in subagent context - this is a main agent TodoWrite, skip
   416→		return nil
   417→	}
   418→
   419→	// Skip on default branch to avoid polluting main/master history
   420→	if skip, branchName := ShouldSkipOnDefaultBranch(); skip {
   421→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping incremental checkpoint on branch '%s'\n", branchName)
   422→		return nil
   423→	}
   424→
   425→	// Detect file changes since last checkpoint
   426→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(nil)
   427→	if err != nil {
   428→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to detect changed files: %v\n", err)
   429→		return nil
   430→	}
   431→
   432→	// If no file changes, skip creating a checkpoint
   433→	if len(changes.Modified) == 0 && len(changes.New) == 0 && len(changes.Deleted) == 0 {
   434→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] No file changes detected, skipping incremental checkpoint\n")
   435→		return nil
   436→	}
   437→
   438→	// Get git author
   439→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   440→	if err != nil {
   441→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to get git author: %v\n", err)
   442→		return nil
   443→	}
   444→
   445→	// Get the active strategy
   446→	strat := GetStrategy()
   447→
   448→	// Ensure strategy setup is complete
   449→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   450→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
   451→		return nil
   452→	}
   453→
   454→	// Get the session ID from the transcript path or input, then transform to Entire session ID
   455→	sessionID := input.SessionID
   456→	if sessionID == "" {
   457→		sessionID = paths.ExtractSessionIDFromTranscriptPath(input.TranscriptPath)
   458→	}
   459→
   460→	// Get next checkpoint sequence
   461→	seq := GetNextCheckpointSequence(sessionID, taskToolUseID)
   462→
   463→	// Extract the todo content from the tool_input.
   464→	// PostToolUse receives the NEW todo list where the just-completed work is
   465→	// marked as "completed". The last completed item is the work that was just done.
   466→	todoContent := ExtractLastCompletedTodoFromToolInput(input.ToolInput)
   467→	if todoContent == "" {
   468→		// No completed items - this is likely the first TodoWrite (planning phase).
   469→		// Check if there are any todos at all to avoid duplicate messages.
   470→		todoCount := CountTodosFromToolInput(input.ToolInput)
   471→		if todoCount > 0 {
   472→			// Use "Planning: N todos" format for the first TodoWrite
   473→			todoContent = fmt.Sprintf("Planning: %d todos", todoCount)
   474→		}
   475→		// If todoCount == 0, todoContent remains empty and FormatIncrementalMessage
   476→		// will fall back to "Checkpoint #N" format
   477→	}
   478→
   479→	// Get agent type from the currently executing hook agent (authoritative source)
   480→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   481→	if hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent(); agentErr == nil {
   482→		agentType = hookAgent.Type()
   483→	}
   484→
   485→	// Build incremental checkpoint context
   486→	ctx := strategy.TaskCheckpointContext{
   487→		SessionID:           sessionID,
   488→		ToolUseID:           taskToolUseID,
   489→		ModifiedFiles:       changes.Modified,
   490→		NewFiles:            changes.New,
   491→		DeletedFiles:        changes.Deleted,
   492→		TranscriptPath:      input.TranscriptPath,
   493→		AuthorName:          author.Name,
   494→		AuthorEmail:         author.Email,
   495→		IsIncremental:       true,
   496→		IncrementalSequence: seq,
   497→		IncrementalType:     input.ToolName,
   498→		IncrementalData:     input.ToolInput,
   499→		TodoContent:         todoContent,
   500→		AgentType:           agentType,
   501→	}
   502→
   503→	// Save incremental checkpoint
   504→	if err := strat.SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx); err != nil {
   505→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to save incremental checkpoint: %v\n", err)
   506→		return nil
   507→	}
   508→
   509→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Created incremental checkpoint #%d for %s (task: %s)\n",
   510→		seq, input.ToolName, taskToolUseID[:min(12, len(taskToolUseID))])
   511→	return nil
   512→}
   513→
   514→// handleClaudeCodePreTask handles the PreToolUse[Task] hook
   515→func handleClaudeCodePreTask() error {
   516→	input, err := parseTaskHookInput(os.Stdin)
   517→	if err != nil {
   518→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PreToolUse[Task] input: %w", err)
   519→	}
   520→
   521→	// Get agent for logging context
   522→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   523→	if err != nil {
   524→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   525→	}
   526→
   527→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   528→	logging.Info(logCtx, "pre-task",
   529→		slog.String("hook", "pre-task"),
   530→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
   531→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   532→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.TranscriptPath),
   533→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
   534→	)
   535→
   536→	// Log context to stdout
   537→	logPreTaskHookContext(os.Stdout, input)
   538→
   539→	// Capture pre-task state locally (for computing new files when task completes).
   540→	// We don't create a shadow branch commit here. Commits are created during
   541→	// task completion (handleClaudeCodePostTask/handleClaudeCodePostTodo) only if the task resulted
   542→	// in file changes.
   543→	if err := CapturePreTaskState(input.ToolUseID); err != nil {
   544→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to capture pre-task state: %w", err)
   545→	}
   546→
   547→	return nil
   548→}
   549→
   550→// handleClaudeCodePostTask handles the PostToolUse[Task] hook
   551→func handleClaudeCodePostTask() error {
   552→	input, err := parsePostTaskHookInput(os.Stdin)
   553→	if err != nil {
   554→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PostToolUse[Task] input: %w", err)
   555→	}
   556→
   557→	// Extract subagent type from tool_input for logging
   558→	subagentType, taskDescription := ParseSubagentTypeAndDescription(input.ToolInput)
   559→
   560→	// Get agent for logging context
   561→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   562→	if err != nil {
   563→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   564→	}
   565→
   566→	// Log parsed input context
   567→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   568→	logging.Info(logCtx, "post-task",
   569→		slog.String("hook", "post-task"),
   570→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
   571→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
   572→		slog.String("agent_id", input.AgentID),
   573→		slog.String("subagent_type", subagentType),
   574→	)
   575→
   576→	// Determine subagent transcript path
   577→	transcriptDir := filepath.Dir(input.TranscriptPath)
   578→	var subagentTranscriptPath string
   579→	if input.AgentID != "" {
   580→		subagentTranscriptPath = AgentTranscriptPath(transcriptDir, input.AgentID)
   581→		if !fileExists(subagentTranscriptPath) {
   582→			subagentTranscriptPath = ""
   583→		}
   584→	}
   585→
   586→	// Log context to stdout
   587→	logPostTaskHookContext(os.Stdout, input, subagentTranscriptPath)
   588→
   589→	// Parse transcript to extract modified files
   590→	var modifiedFiles []string
   591→	if subagentTranscriptPath != "" {
   592→		// Use subagent transcript if available
   593→		transcript, err := parseTranscript(subagentTranscriptPath)
   594→		if err != nil {
   595→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to parse subagent transcript: %v\n", err)
   596→		} else {
   597→			modifiedFiles = extractModifiedFiles(transcript)
   598→		}
   599→	} else {
   600→		// Fall back to main transcript
   601→		transcript, err := parseTranscript(input.TranscriptPath)
   602→		if err != nil {
   603→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to parse transcript: %v\n", err)
   604→		} else {
   605→			modifiedFiles = extractModifiedFiles(transcript)
   606→		}
   607→	}
   608→
   609→	// Load pre-task state and compute new files
   610→	preState, err := LoadPreTaskState(input.ToolUseID)
   611→	if err != nil {
   612→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-task state: %v\n", err)
   613→	}
   614→
   615→	// Compute new and deleted files (single git status call)
   616→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preState.PreUntrackedFiles())
   617→	if err != nil {
   618→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   619→	}
   620→
   621→	// Get repo root for path conversion (not cwd, since Claude may be in a subdirectory)
   622→	// Using cwd would filter out files in sibling directories (paths starting with ..)
   623→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   624→	if err != nil {
   625→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   626→	}
   627→
   628→	// Filter and normalize paths
   629→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   630→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   631→	if changes != nil {
   632→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   633→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   634→	}
   635→
   636→	// If no file changes, skip creating a checkpoint
   637→	if len(relModifiedFiles) == 0 && len(relNewFiles) == 0 && len(relDeletedFiles) == 0 {
   638→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] No file changes detected, skipping task checkpoint\n")
   639→		// Cleanup pre-task state (ignore error - cleanup is best-effort)
   640→		_ = CleanupPreTaskState(input.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   641→		return nil
   642→	}
   643→
   644→	// Find checkpoint UUID from main transcript (best-effort, ignore errors)
   645→	transcript, _ := parseTranscript(input.TranscriptPath) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort extraction
   646→	checkpointUUID, _ := FindCheckpointUUID(transcript, input.ToolUseID)
   647→
   648→	// Get git author
   649→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   650→	if err != nil {
   651→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   652→	}
   653→
   654→	// Get the configured strategy
   655→	strat := GetStrategy()
   656→
   657→	// Ensure strategy setup is in place
   658→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   659→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
   660→	}
   661→
   662→	// Get agent type from the currently executing hook agent (authoritative source)
   663→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   664→	if hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent(); agentErr == nil {
   665→		agentType = hookAgent.Type()
   666→	}
   667→
   668→	// Build task checkpoint context - strategy handles metadata creation
   669→	// Note: Incremental checkpoints are now created during task execution via handleClaudeCodePostTodo,
   670→	// so we don't need to collect/cleanup staging area here.
   671→	ctx := strategy.TaskCheckpointContext{
   672→		SessionID:              input.SessionID,
   673→		ToolUseID:              input.ToolUseID,
   674→		AgentID:                input.AgentID,
   675→		ModifiedFiles:          relModifiedFiles,
   676→		NewFiles:               relNewFiles,
   677→		DeletedFiles:           relDeletedFiles,
   678→		TranscriptPath:         input.TranscriptPath,
   679→		SubagentTranscriptPath: subagentTranscriptPath,
   680→		CheckpointUUID:         checkpointUUID,
   681→		AuthorName:             author.Name,
   682→		AuthorEmail:            author.Email,
   683→		SubagentType:           subagentType,
   684→		TaskDescription:        taskDescription,
   685→		AgentType:              agentType,
   686→	}
   687→
   688→	// Call strategy to save task checkpoint - strategy handles all metadata creation
   689→	if err := strat.SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx); err != nil {
   690→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save task checkpoint: %w", err)
   691→	}
   692→
   693→	// Cleanup pre-task state (ignore error - cleanup is best-effort)
   694→	_ = CleanupPreTaskState(input.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   695→
   696→	return nil
   697→}
   698→
   699→// handleClaudeCodeSessionStart handles the SessionStart hook for Claude Code.
   700→func handleClaudeCodeSessionStart() error {
   701→	return handleSessionStartCommon()
   702→}
   703→
   704→// handleClaudeCodeSessionEnd handles the SessionEnd hook for Claude Code.
   705→// This fires when the user explicitly closes the session.
   706→// Updates the session state with EndedAt timestamp.
   707→func handleClaudeCodeSessionEnd() error {
   708→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   709→	if err != nil {
   710→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   711→	}
   712→
   713→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookSessionEnd, os.Stdin)
   714→	if err != nil {
   715→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   716→	}
   717→
   718→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   719→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-end",
   720→		slog.String("hook", "session-end"),
   721→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
   722→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   723→	)
   724→
   725→	if input.SessionID == "" {
   726→		return nil // No session to update
   727→	}
   728→
   729→	// Best-effort cleanup - don't block session closure on failure
   730→	if err := markSessionEnded(input.SessionID); err != nil {
   731→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to mark session ended: %v\n", err)
   732→	}
   733→	return nil
   734→}
   735→
   736→// transitionSessionTurnEnd fires EventTurnEnd to move the session from
   737→// ACTIVE → IDLE (or ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE). Best-effort: logs warnings
   738→// on failure rather than returning errors.
   739→func transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID string) {
   740→	turnState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   741→	if loadErr != nil {
   742→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state for turn end: %v\n", loadErr)
   743→		return
   744→	}
   745→	if turnState == nil {
   746→		return
   747→	}
   748→	remaining := strategy.TransitionAndLog(turnState, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{})
   749→
   750→	// Dispatch strategy-specific actions (e.g., ActionCondense for ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE)
   751→	if len(remaining) > 0 {
   752→		strat := GetStrategy()
   753→		if handler, ok := strat.(strategy.TurnEndHandler); ok {
   754→			if err := handler.HandleTurnEnd(turnState, remaining); err != nil {
   755→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: turn-end action dispatch failed: %v\n", err)
   756→			}
   757→		}
   758→	}
   759→
   760→	if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(turnState); updateErr != nil {
   761→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session phase on turn end: %v\n", updateErr)
   762→	}
   763→}
   764→
   765→// markSessionEnded transitions the session to ENDED phase via the state machine.
   766→func markSessionEnded(sessionID string) error {
   767→	state, err := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   768→	if err != nil {
   769→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
   770→	}
   771→	if state == nil {
   772→		return nil // No state file, nothing to update
   773→	}
   774→
   775→	strategy.TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventSessionStop, session.TransitionContext{})
   776→
   777→	now := time.Now()
   778→	state.EndedAt = &now
   779→
   780→	if err := strategy.SaveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   781→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   782→	}
   783→	return nil
   784→}
   785→
   786→// stopHookSentinel is the string that appears in Claude Code's hook_progress
   787→// transcript entry when it launches our stop hook. Used to detect that the
   788→// transcript file has been fully flushed before we copy it.
   789→const stopHookSentinel = "hooks claude-code stop"
   790→
   791→// waitForTranscriptFlush polls the transcript file tail for the hook_progress
   792→// sentinel entry that Claude Code writes when launching the stop hook.
   793→// Once this entry appears in the file, all prior entries (assistant replies,
   794→// tool results) are guaranteed to have been flushed.
   795→//
   796→// hookStartTime is the approximate time our process started, used to avoid
   797→// matching stale sentinel entries from previous stop hook invocations.
   798→//
   799→// Falls back silently after a timeout — the transcript copy will proceed
   800→// with whatever data is available.
   801→func waitForTranscriptFlush(transcriptPath string, hookStartTime time.Time) {
   802→	const (
   803→		maxWait      = 3 * time.Second
   804→		pollInterval = 50 * time.Millisecond
   805→		tailBytes    = 4096 // Read last 4KB — sentinel is near the end
   806→		maxSkew      = 2 * time.Second
   807→	)
   808→
   809→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks")
   810→	deadline := time.Now().Add(maxWait)
   811→	for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
   812→		if checkStopSentinel(transcriptPath, tailBytes, hookStartTime, maxSkew) {
   813→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel found",
   814→				slog.Duration("wait", time.Since(hookStartTime)),
   815→			)
   816→			return
   817→		}
   818→		time.Sleep(pollInterval)
   819→	}
   820→	// Timeout — proceed with whatever is on disk.
   821→	logging.Warn(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel not found within timeout, proceeding",
   822→		slog.Duration("timeout", maxWait),
   823→	)
   824→}
   825→
   826→// checkStopSentinel reads the tail of the transcript file and looks for a
   827→// hook_progress entry containing the stop hook sentinel, with a timestamp
   828→// close to hookStartTime.
   829→func checkStopSentinel(path string, tailBytes int64, hookStartTime time.Time, maxSkew time.Duration) bool {
   830→	f, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // path comes from agent hook input
   831→	if err != nil {
   832→		return false
   833→	}
   834→	defer f.Close()
   835→
   836→	// Seek to tail
   837→	info, err := f.Stat()
   838→	if err != nil {
   839→		return false
   840→	}
   841→	offset := info.Size() - tailBytes
   842→	if offset < 0 {
   843→		offset = 0
   844→	}
   845→	buf := make([]byte, info.Size()-offset)
   846→	if _, err := f.ReadAt(buf, offset); err != nil {
   847→		return false
   848→	}
   849→
   850→	// Scan lines from the tail for the sentinel
   851→	lines := strings.Split(string(buf), "\n")
   852→	for _, line := range lines {
   853→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   854→		if line == "" || !strings.Contains(line, stopHookSentinel) {
   855→			continue
   856→		}
   857→
   858→		// Parse timestamp to check recency
   859→		var entry struct {
   860→			Timestamp string `json:"timestamp"`
   861→		}
   862→		if json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &entry) != nil || entry.Timestamp == "" {
   863→			continue
   864→		}
   865→		ts, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, entry.Timestamp)
   866→		if err != nil {
   867→			ts, err = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, entry.Timestamp)
   868→			if err != nil {
   869→				continue
   870→			}
   871→		}
   872→
   873→		// Check timestamp is within skew window of our start time
   874→		if ts.After(hookStartTime.Add(-maxSkew)) && ts.Before(hookStartTime.Add(maxSkew)) {
   875→			return true
   876→		}
   877→	}
   878→	return false
   879→}
   880→
```

```
    68→			// Check if we're in a git repository first - this is a prerequisite error,

    70→			// to prevent duplicate error output in main.go

   134→func newDisableCmd() *cobra.Command {
   135→	var useProjectSettings bool
   136→	var uninstall bool
   137→	var force bool
   138→
   139→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
   140→		Use:   "disable",

   145→show a disabled message.
   146→
   147→To completely remove Entire integrations from this repository, use --uninstall:
   148→  - .entire/ directory (settings, logs, metadata)
   149→  - Git hooks (prepare-commit-msg, commit-msg, post-commit, pre-push)
   150→  - Session state files (.git/entire-sessions/)
   151→  - Shadow branches (entire/<hash>)
   152→  - Agent hooks (Claude Code, Gemini CLI)`,

   154→			if uninstall {
   155→				return runUninstall(cmd.OutOrStdout(), cmd.ErrOrStderr(), force)
   156→			}
   157→			return runDisable(cmd.OutOrStdout(), useProjectSettings)
   158→		},
   159→	}

   163→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&force, "force", false, "Skip confirmation prompt (use with --uninstall)")

   168→// isFullyEnabled checks whether Entire is already fully set up.
   169→// Returns whether it's fully enabled, and if so, the agent type display name and config file path.
   170→func isFullyEnabled() (enabled bool, agentDesc string, configPath string) {
   171→	// Check settings exist and Enabled == true
   172→	s, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   173→	if err != nil || !s.Enabled {
   174→		return false, "", ""
   175→	}
   176→
   177→	// Check any agent hooks installed (not just Claude Code — works with Gemini too)
   178→	installedAgents := GetAgentsWithHooksInstalled()
   179→	if len(installedAgents) == 0 {
   180→		return false, "", ""
   181→	}
   182→
   183→	// Check git hooks installed
   184→	if !strategy.IsGitHookInstalled() {
   185→		return false, "", ""
   186→	}
   187→
   188→	// Check .entire directory exists
   189→	if !checkEntireDirExists() {
   190→		return false, "", ""
   191→	}
   192→
   193→	// Determine agent description from first installed agent
   194→	desc := string(installedAgents[0]) // fallback to agent name
   195→	if ag, err := agent.Get(installedAgents[0]); err == nil {
   196→		desc = string(ag.Type())
   197→	}
   198→
   199→	// Determine config path - check if local settings exists, otherwise show project settings
   200→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
   201→	if err != nil {
   202→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir
   203→	}
   204→	configDisplay := configDisplayProject
   205→	localSettingsPath := filepath.Join(entireDirAbs, "settings.local.json")
   206→	if _, err := os.Stat(localSettingsPath); err == nil {
   207→		configDisplay = configDisplayLocal
   208→	}
   209→
   210→	return true, desc, configDisplay
   211→}
   212→
   213→// runEnableWithStrategy enables Entire with a specified strategy (non-interactive).
   214→// The selectedStrategy can be either a display name (manual-commit, auto-commit)
   215→// or an internal name (manual-commit, auto-commit).
   216→func runEnableWithStrategy(w io.Writer, selectedStrategy string, localDev, _, useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings, forceHooks, skipPushSessions, telemetry bool) error {
   217→	// Map the strategy to internal name if it's a display name
   218→	internalStrategy := selectedStrategy
   219→	if mapped, ok := strategyDisplayToInternal[selectedStrategy]; ok {
   220→		internalStrategy = mapped
   221→	}
   222→
   223→	// Validate the strategy exists
   224→	strat, err := strategy.Get(internalStrategy)
   225→	if err != nil {
   226→		return fmt.Errorf("unknown strategy: %s (use manual-commit or auto-commit)", selectedStrategy)
   227→	}
   228→
   229→	// Detect default agent
   230→	ag := agent.Default()
   231→	agentType := string(agent.AgentTypeClaudeCode)
   232→	if ag != nil {
   233→		agentType = string(ag.Type())
   234→	}
   235→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "Agent: %s (use --agent to change)\n\n", agentType)
   236→
   237→	// Setup Claude Code hooks (agent hooks don't depend on settings)
   238→	if _, err := setupClaudeCodeHook(localDev, forceHooks); err != nil {
   239→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup Claude Code hooks: %w", err)
   240→	}
   241→
   242→	// Setup .entire directory
   243→	if _, err := setupEntireDirectory(); err != nil {
   244→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup .entire directory: %w", err)
   245→	}
   246→
   247→	// Load existing settings to preserve other options (like strategy_options.push)
   248→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   249→	if err != nil {
   250→		// If we can't load, start with defaults
   251→		settings = &EntireSettings{}
   252→	}
   253→	// Update the specific fields
   254→	settings.Strategy = internalStrategy
   255→	settings.LocalDev = localDev
   256→	settings.Enabled = true
   257→
   258→	// Set push_sessions option if --skip-push-sessions flag was provided
   259→	if skipPushSessions {
   260→		if settings.StrategyOptions == nil {
   261→			settings.StrategyOptions = make(map[string]interface{})
   262→		}
   263→		settings.StrategyOptions["push_sessions"] = false
   264→	}
   265→
   266→	// Handle telemetry for non-interactive mode
   267→	// Note: if telemetry is nil (not configured), it defaults to disabled
   268→	if !telemetry || os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT") != "" {
   269→		f := false
   270→		settings.Telemetry = &f
   271→	}
   272→
   273→	// Determine which settings file to write to
   274→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
   275→	if err != nil {
   276→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir // Fallback to relative
   277→	}
   278→	shouldUseLocal, showNotification := determineSettingsTarget(entireDirAbs, useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings)
   279→
   280→	if showNotification {
   281→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "Info: Project settings exist. Saving to settings.local.json instead.")
   282→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Use --project to update the project settings file.")
   283→	}
   284→
   285→	configDisplay := configDisplayProject
   286→	if shouldUseLocal {
   287→		if err := SaveEntireSettingsLocal(settings); err != nil {
   288→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save local settings: %w", err)
   289→		}
   290→		configDisplay = configDisplayLocal
   291→	} else {
   292→		if err := SaveEntireSettings(settings); err != nil {
   293→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   294→		}
   295→	}
   296→
   297→	// Install git hooks AFTER saving settings (InstallGitHook reads local_dev from settings)
   298→	if _, err := strategy.InstallGitHook(true); err != nil {
   299→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to install git hooks: %w", err)
   300→	}
   301→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Hooks installed")
   302→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "✓ Project configured (%s)\n", configDisplay)
   303→
   304→	// Let the strategy handle its own setup requirements
   305→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   306→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup strategy: %w", err)
   307→	}
   308→
   309→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nReady.")
   310→
   311→	return nil
   312→}
   313→
   314→// runEnableInteractive runs the interactive enable flow.
   315→func runEnableInteractive(w io.Writer, localDev, _, useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings, forceHooks, skipPushSessions, telemetry bool) error {
   316→	// Check if already fully enabled — show summary and return early.
   317→	// Skip early return if any configuration flags are set (user wants to reconfigure).
   318→	hasConfigFlags := forceHooks || skipPushSessions || !telemetry || useLocalSettings || useProjectSettings || localDev
   319→	if !hasConfigFlags {
   320→		if fullyEnabled, agentDesc, configPath := isFullyEnabled(); fullyEnabled {
   321→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "Already enabled. Everything looks good.")
   322→			fmt.Fprintln(w)
   323→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Agent: %s\n", agentDesc)
   324→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Config: %s\n", configPath)
   325→			return nil
   326→		}
   327→	}
   328→
   329→	// Detect default agent
   330→	ag := agent.Default()
   331→	agentType := string(agent.AgentTypeClaudeCode)
   332→	if ag != nil {
   333→		agentType = string(ag.Type())
   334→	}
   335→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "Agent: %s (use --agent to change)\n\n", agentType)
   336→
   337→	// Setup Claude Code hooks (agent hooks don't depend on settings)
   338→	if _, err := setupClaudeCodeHook(localDev, forceHooks); err != nil {
   339→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup Claude Code hooks: %w", err)
   340→	}
   341→
   342→	// Setup .entire directory
   343→	if _, err := setupEntireDirectory(); err != nil {
   344→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup .entire directory: %w", err)
   345→	}
   346→
   347→	// Use the default strategy (manual-commit)
   348→	internalStrategy := strategy.DefaultStrategyName
   349→
   350→	// Load existing settings to preserve other options (like strategy_options.push)
   351→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   352→	if err != nil {
   353→		// If we can't load, start with defaults
   354→		settings = &EntireSettings{}
   355→	}
   356→	// Update the specific fields
   357→	settings.Strategy = internalStrategy
   358→	settings.LocalDev = localDev
   359→	settings.Enabled = true
   360→
   361→	// Set push_sessions option if --skip-push-sessions flag was provided
   362→	if skipPushSessions {
   363→		if settings.StrategyOptions == nil {
   364→			settings.StrategyOptions = make(map[string]interface{})
   365→		}
   366→		settings.StrategyOptions["push_sessions"] = false
   367→	}
   368→
   369→	// Determine which settings file to write to
   370→	// First run always creates settings.json (no prompt)
   371→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
   372→	if err != nil {
   373→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir // Fallback to relative
   374→	}
   375→	shouldUseLocal, showNotification := determineSettingsTarget(entireDirAbs, useLocalSettings, useProjectSettings)
   376→
   377→	if showNotification {
   378→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "Info: Project settings exist. Saving to settings.local.json instead.")
   379→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Use --project to update the project settings file.")
   380→	}
   381→
   382→	// Helper to save settings to the appropriate file
   383→	saveSettings := func() error {
   384→		if shouldUseLocal {
   385→			return SaveEntireSettingsLocal(settings)
   386→		}
   387→		return SaveEntireSettings(settings)
   388→	}
   389→
   390→	// Save settings before telemetry prompt so config is persisted even if the user cancels
   391→	if err := saveSettings(); err != nil {
   392→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   393→	}
   394→
   395→	// Install git hooks AFTER saving settings (InstallGitHook reads local_dev from settings)
   396→	if _, err := strategy.InstallGitHook(true); err != nil {
   397→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to install git hooks: %w", err)
   398→	}
   399→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "✓ Hooks installed")
   400→
   401→	configDisplay := configDisplayProject
   402→	if shouldUseLocal {
   403→		configDisplay = configDisplayLocal
   404→	}
   405→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "✓ Project configured (%s)\n", configDisplay)
   406→
   407→	// Ask about telemetry consent (only if not already asked)
   408→	fmt.Fprintln(w)
   409→	if err := promptTelemetryConsent(settings, telemetry); err != nil {
   410→		return fmt.Errorf("telemetry consent: %w", err)
   411→	}
   412→	// Save again to persist telemetry choice
   413→	if err := saveSettings(); err != nil {
   414→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   415→	}
   416→
   417→	// Let the strategy handle its own setup requirements
   418→	strat, err := strategy.Get(internalStrategy)
   419→	if err != nil {
   420→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get strategy: %w", err)
   421→	}
   422→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   423→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup strategy: %w", err)
   424→	}
   425→
   426→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nReady.")
   427→
   428→	return nil
   429→}
   430→
   431→// runEnable is a simple enable that just sets the enabled flag (for programmatic use).
   432→func runEnable(w io.Writer) error {
   433→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   434→	if err != nil {
   435→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load settings: %w", err)
   436→	}
   437→
   438→	settings.Enabled = true
   439→	if err := SaveEntireSettings(settings); err != nil {
   440→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   441→	}
   442→
   443→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "Entire is now enabled.")
   444→	return nil
   445→}
   446→
   447→func runDisable(w io.Writer, useProjectSettings bool) error {
   448→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   449→	if err != nil {
   450→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load settings: %w", err)
   451→	}
   452→
   453→	settings.Enabled = false
   454→
   455→	// If --project flag is specified, always write to project settings
   456→	if useProjectSettings {
   457→		if err := SaveEntireSettings(settings); err != nil {
   458→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   459→		}
   460→	} else {
   461→		// Always write to local settings file (create if doesn't exist)
   462→		if err := SaveEntireSettingsLocal(settings); err != nil {
   463→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to save local settings: %w", err)
   464→		}
   465→	}
   466→
   467→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "Entire is now disabled.")
   468→	return nil
   469→}
   470→
   471→// DisabledMessage is the message shown when Entire is disabled
   472→const DisabledMessage = "Entire is disabled. Run `entire enable` to re-enable."
   473→
   474→// checkDisabledGuard checks if Entire is disabled and prints a message if so.
   475→// Returns true if the caller should exit (i.e., Entire is disabled).
   476→// On error reading settings, defaults to enabled (returns false).
   477→func checkDisabledGuard(w io.Writer) bool {
   478→	enabled, err := IsEnabled()
   479→	if err != nil {
   480→		// Default to enabled on error
   481→		return false
   482→	}
   483→	if !enabled {
   484→		fmt.Fprintln(w, DisabledMessage)
   485→		return true
   486→	}
   487→	return false
   488→}
   489→
   490→// setupClaudeCodeHook sets up Claude Code hooks.
   491→// This is a convenience wrapper that uses the agent package.
   492→// Returns the number of hooks installed (0 if already installed).
   493→func setupClaudeCodeHook(localDev, forceHooks bool) (int, error) { //nolint:unparam // already present in codebase
   494→	ag, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentNameClaudeCode)
   495→	if err != nil {
   496→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to get claude-code agent: %w", err)
   497→	}
   498→
   499→	hookAgent, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport)
   500→	if !ok {
   501→		return 0, errors.New("claude-code agent does not support hooks")
   502→	}
   503→
   504→	count, err := hookAgent.InstallHooks(localDev, forceHooks)
   505→	if err != nil {
   506→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to install claude-code hooks: %w", err)
   507→	}
   508→
   509→	return count, nil
   510→}
   511→
   512→// printAgentError writes an error message followed by available agents and usage.
   513→func printAgentError(w io.Writer, message string) {
   514→	agents := agent.List()
   515→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "%s Available agents:\n", message)
   516→	fmt.Fprintln(w)
   517→	for _, a := range agents {
   518→		suffix := ""
   519→		if a == agent.DefaultAgentName {
   520→			suffix = "    (default)"
   521→		}
   522→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  %s%s\n", a, suffix)
   523→	}
   524→	fmt.Fprintln(w)
   525→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "Usage: entire enable --agent <agent-name>")
   526→}
   527→
   528→// printMissingAgentError writes a helpful error listing available agents.
   529→func printMissingAgentError(w io.Writer) {
   530→	printAgentError(w, "Missing agent name.")
   531→}
   532→
   533→// printWrongAgentError writes a helpful error when an unknown agent name is provided.
   534→func printWrongAgentError(w io.Writer, name string) {
   535→	printAgentError(w, fmt.Sprintf("Unknown agent %q.", name))
   536→}
   537→
   538→// setupAgentHooksNonInteractive sets up hooks for a specific agent non-interactively.
   539→// If strategyName is provided, it sets the strategy; otherwise uses default.
   540→func setupAgentHooksNonInteractive(w io.Writer, ag agent.Agent, strategyName string, localDev, forceHooks, skipPushSessions, telemetry bool) error {
   541→	agentName := ag.Name()
   542→	// Check if agent supports hooks
   543→	hookAgent, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport)
   544→	if !ok {
   545→		return fmt.Errorf("agent %s does not support hooks", agentName)
   546→	}
   547→
   548→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "Agent: %s\n\n", ag.Type())
   549→
   550→	// Install agent hooks (agent hooks don't depend on settings)
   551→	installedHooks, err := hookAgent.InstallHooks(localDev, forceHooks)
   552→	if err != nil {
   553→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to install hooks for %s: %w", agentName, err)
   554→	}
   555→
   556→	// Setup .entire directory
   557→	if _, err := setupEntireDirectory(); err != nil {
   558→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup .entire directory: %w", err)
   559→	}
   560→
   561→	// Load existing settings to preserve other options (like strategy_options.push)
   562→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
   563→	if err != nil {
   564→		// If we can't load, start with defaults
   565→		settings = &EntireSettings{Strategy: strategy.DefaultStrategyName}
   566→	}
   567→	settings.Enabled = true
   568→	if localDev {
   569→		settings.LocalDev = localDev
   570→	}
   571→
   572→	// Set push_sessions option if --skip-push-sessions flag was provided
   573→	if skipPushSessions {
   574→		if settings.StrategyOptions == nil {
   575→			settings.StrategyOptions = make(map[string]interface{})
   576→		}
   577→		settings.StrategyOptions["push_sessions"] = false
   578→	}
   579→
   580→	// Set strategy if provided
   581→	if strategyName != "" {
   582→		// Map display name to internal name if needed
   583→		internalStrategy := strategyName
   584→		if mapped, ok := strategyDisplayToInternal[strategyName]; ok {
   585→			internalStrategy = mapped
   586→		}
   587→		// Validate the strategy exists
   588→		if _, err := strategy.Get(internalStrategy); err != nil {
   589→			return fmt.Errorf("unknown strategy: %s (use manual-commit or auto-commit)", strategyName)
   590→		}
   591→		settings.Strategy = internalStrategy
   592→	}
   593→
   594→	// Handle telemetry for non-interactive mode
   595→	// Note: if telemetry is nil (not configured), it defaults to disabled
   596→	if !telemetry || os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT") != "" {
   597→		f := false
   598→		settings.Telemetry = &f
   599→	}
   600→
   601→	if err := SaveEntireSettings(settings); err != nil {
   602→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
   603→	}
   604→
   605→	// Install git hooks AFTER saving settings (InstallGitHook reads local_dev from settings)
   606→	if _, err := strategy.InstallGitHook(true); err != nil {
   607→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to install git hooks: %w", err)
   608→	}
   609→
   610→	if installedHooks == 0 {
   611→		msg := fmt.Sprintf("Hooks for %s already installed", ag.Description())
   612→		if agentName == agent.AgentNameGemini {
   613→			msg += " (Preview)"
   614→		}
   615→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "%s\n", msg)
   616→	} else {
   617→		msg := fmt.Sprintf("Installed %d hooks for %s", installedHooks, ag.Description())
   618→		if agentName == agent.AgentNameGemini {
   619→			msg += " (Preview)"
   620→		}
   621→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "%s\n", msg)
   622→	}
   623→
   624→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "✓ Project configured (%s)\n", configDisplayProject)
   625→
   626→	// Let the strategy handle its own setup requirements (creates entire/checkpoints/v1 branch, etc.)
   627→	strat, err := strategy.Get(settings.Strategy)
   628→	if err != nil {
   629→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get strategy: %w", err)
   630→	}
   631→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   632→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to setup strategy: %w", err)
   633→	}
   634→
   635→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nReady.")
   636→
   637→	return nil
   638→}
   639→
   640→// validateSetupFlags checks that --local and --project flags are not both specified.
   641→func validateSetupFlags(useLocal, useProject bool) error {
   642→	if useLocal && useProject {
   643→		return errors.New("cannot specify both --project and --local")
   644→	}
   645→	return nil
   646→}
   647→
   648→// determineSettingsTarget decides whether to write to settings.local.json based on:
   649→// - Whether settings.json already exists
   650→// - The --local and --project flags
   651→// Returns (useLocal, showNotification).
   652→func determineSettingsTarget(entireDir string, useLocal, useProject bool) (bool, bool) {
   653→	// Explicit --local flag always uses local settings
   654→	if useLocal {
   655→		return true, false
   656→	}
   657→
   658→	// Explicit --project flag always uses project settings
   659→	if useProject {
   660→		return false, false
   661→	}
   662→
   663→	// No flags specified - check if settings file exists
   664→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(entireDir, paths.SettingsFileName)
   665→	if _, err := os.Stat(settingsPath); err == nil {
   666→		// Settings file exists - auto-redirect to local with notification
   667→		return true, true
   668→	}
   669→
   670→	// Settings file doesn't exist - create it
   671→	return false, false
   672→}
   673→
   674→// setupEntireDirectory creates the .entire directory and gitignore.
   675→// Returns true if the directory was created, false if it already existed.
   676→func setupEntireDirectory() (bool, error) { //nolint:unparam // already present in codebase
   677→	// Get absolute path for the .entire directory
   678→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
   679→	if err != nil {
   680→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir // Fallback to relative
   681→	}
   682→
   683→	// Check if directory already exists
   684→	created := false
   685→	if _, err := os.Stat(entireDirAbs); os.IsNotExist(err) {
   686→		created = true
   687→	}
   688→
   689→	// Create .entire directory
   690→	//nolint:gosec // G301: Project directory needs standard permissions for git
   691→	if err := os.MkdirAll(entireDirAbs, 0o755); err != nil {
   692→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to create .entire directory: %w", err)
   693→	}
   694→
   695→	// Create/update .gitignore with all required entries
   696→	if err := strategy.EnsureEntireGitignore(); err != nil {
   697→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to setup .gitignore: %w", err)
   698→	}
   699→
   700→	return created, nil
   701→}
   702→
   703→// setupGitHook installs the prepare-commit-msg hook for context trailers.
   704→func setupGitHook() error {
   705→	// Use shared implementation from strategy package
   706→	// The localDev setting is read from settings.json
   707→	_, err := strategy.InstallGitHook(false) // not silent - show output during setup
   708→	if err != nil {
   709→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to install git hook: %w", err)
   710→	}
   711→	return nil
   712→}

   714→// newSetupGitHookCmd creates the standalone git-hook setup command
   715→func newSetupGitHookCmd() *cobra.Command {
   716→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
   717→		Use:    "git-hook",
   718→		Short:  "Install git hook for session context trailers",
   719→		Hidden: true, // Hidden as it's mainly for testing
   720→		RunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   721→			return setupGitHook()
   722→		},
   723→	}
   724→
   725→	return cmd
   726→}
   727→
   728→func newCurlBashPostInstallCmd() *cobra.Command {
   729→	return &cobra.Command{
   730→		Use:    "curl-bash-post-install",
   731→		Short:  "Post-install tasks for curl|bash installer",
   732→		Hidden: true,
   733→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   734→			w := cmd.OutOrStdout()
   735→			if err := promptShellCompletion(w); err != nil {
   736→				fmt.Fprintf(w, "Note: Shell completion setup skipped: %v\n", err)
   737→			}
   738→			return nil
   739→		},
   740→	}
   741→}
   742→
   743→// shellCompletionComment is the comment preceding the completion line
   744→const shellCompletionComment = "# Entire CLI shell completion"
   745→
   746→// errUnsupportedShell is returned when the user's shell is not supported for completion.
   747→var errUnsupportedShell = errors.New("unsupported shell")
   748→
   749→// shellCompletionTarget returns the rc file path and completion lines for the
   750→// user's current shell.
   751→func shellCompletionTarget() (shellName, rcFile, completionLine string, err error) {
   752→	home, err := os.UserHomeDir()
   753→	if err != nil {
   754→		return "", "", "", fmt.Errorf("cannot determine home directory: %w", err)
   755→	}
   756→
   757→	shell := os.Getenv("SHELL")
   758→	switch {
   759→	case strings.Contains(shell, "zsh"):
   760→		return "Zsh",
   761→			filepath.Join(home, ".zshrc"),
   762→			"autoload -Uz compinit && compinit && source <(entire completion zsh)",
   763→			nil
   764→	case strings.Contains(shell, "bash"):
   765→		bashRC := filepath.Join(home, ".bashrc")
   766→		if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(home, ".bash_profile")); err == nil {
   767→			bashRC = filepath.Join(home, ".bash_profile")
   768→		}
   769→		return "Bash",
   770→			bashRC,
   771→			"source <(entire completion bash)",
   772→			nil
   773→	case strings.Contains(shell, "fish"):
   774→		return "Fish",
   775→			filepath.Join(home, ".config", "fish", "config.fish"),
   776→			"entire completion fish | source",
   777→			nil
   778→	default:
   779→		return "", "", "", errUnsupportedShell
   780→	}
   781→}
   782→
   783→// promptShellCompletion offers to add shell completion to the user's rc file.
   784→// Only prompts if completion is not already configured.
   785→func promptShellCompletion(w io.Writer) error {
   786→	shellName, rcFile, completionLine, err := shellCompletionTarget()
   787→	if err != nil {
   788→		if errors.Is(err, errUnsupportedShell) {
   789→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "Note: Shell completion not available for your shell. Supported: zsh, bash, fish.\n")
   790→			return nil
   791→		}
   792→		return fmt.Errorf("shell completion: %w", err)
   793→	}
   794→
   795→	if isCompletionConfigured(rcFile) {
   796→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "✓ Shell completion already configured in %s\n", rcFile)
   797→		return nil
   798→	}
   799→
   800→	var selected string
   801→	form := NewAccessibleForm(
   802→		huh.NewGroup(
   803→			huh.NewSelect[string]().
   804→				Title(fmt.Sprintf("Enable shell completion? (detected: %s)", shellName)).
   805→				Options(
   806→					huh.NewOption("Yes", "yes"),
   807→					huh.NewOption("No", "no"),
   808→				).
   809→				Value(&selected),
   810→		),
   811→	)
   812→
   813→	if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
   814→		//nolint:nilerr // User cancelled - not a fatal error, just skip
   815→		return nil
   816→	}
   817→
   818→	if selected != "yes" {
   819→		return nil
   820→	}
   821→
   822→	if err := appendShellCompletion(rcFile, completionLine); err != nil {
   823→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to update %s: %w", rcFile, err)
   824→	}
   825→
   826→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "✓ Shell completion added to %s\n", rcFile)
   827→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Restart your shell to activate")
   828→
   829→	return nil
   830→}
   831→
   832→// isCompletionConfigured checks if shell completion is already in the rc file.
   833→func isCompletionConfigured(rcFile string) bool {
   834→	//nolint:gosec // G304: rcFile is constructed from home dir + known filename, not user input
   835→	content, err := os.ReadFile(rcFile)
   836→	if err != nil {
   837→		return false // File doesn't exist or can't read, treat as not configured
   838→	}
   839→	return strings.Contains(string(content), "entire completion")
   840→}
   841→
   842→// appendShellCompletion adds the completion line to the rc file.
   843→func appendShellCompletion(rcFile, completionLine string) error {
   844→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(rcFile), 0o700); err != nil {
   845→		return fmt.Errorf("creating directory: %w", err)
   846→	}
   847→	//nolint:gosec // G302: Shell rc files need 0644 for user readability
   848→	f, err := os.OpenFile(rcFile, os.O_APPEND|os.O_CREATE|os.O_WRONLY, 0o644)
   849→	if err != nil {
   850→		return fmt.Errorf("opening file: %w", err)
   851→	}
   852→	defer f.Close()
   853→
   854→	_, err = f.WriteString("\n" + shellCompletionComment + "\n" + completionLine + "\n")
   855→	if err != nil {
   856→		return fmt.Errorf("writing completion: %w", err)
   857→	}
   858→	return nil
   859→}
   860→
   861→// promptTelemetryConsent asks the user if they want to enable telemetry.
   862→// It modifies settings.Telemetry based on the user's choice or flags.
   863→// The caller is responsible for saving settings.
   864→func promptTelemetryConsent(settings *EntireSettings, telemetryFlag bool) error {
   865→	// Handle --telemetry=false flag first (always overrides existing setting)
   866→	if !telemetryFlag {
   867→		f := false
   868→		settings.Telemetry = &f
   869→		return nil
   870→	}
   871→
   872→	// Skip if already asked
   873→	if settings.Telemetry != nil {
   874→		return nil
   875→	}
   876→
   877→	// Skip if env var disables telemetry (record as disabled)
   878→	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT") != "" {
   879→		f := false
   880→		settings.Telemetry = &f
   881→		return nil
   882→	}
   883→
   884→	consent := true // Default to Yes
   885→	form := NewAccessibleForm(
   886→		huh.NewGroup(
   887→			huh.NewConfirm().
   888→				Title("Help improve Entire CLI?").
   889→				Description("Share anonymous usage data. No code or personal info collected.").
   890→				Affirmative("Yes").
   891→				Negative("No").
   892→				Value(&consent),
   893→		),
   894→	)
   895→
   896→	if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
   897→		return fmt.Errorf("telemetry prompt: %w", err)
   898→	}
   899→
   900→	settings.Telemetry = &consent
   901→	return nil
   902→}
   903→
   904→// runUninstall completely removes Entire from the repository.
   905→func runUninstall(w, errW io.Writer, force bool) error {
   906→	// Check if we're in a git repository
   907→	if _, err := paths.RepoRoot(); err != nil {
   908→		fmt.Fprintln(errW, "Not a git repository. Nothing to uninstall.")
   909→		return NewSilentError(errors.New("not a git repository"))
   910→	}
   911→
   912→	// Gather counts for display
   913→	sessionStateCount := countSessionStates()
   914→	shadowBranchCount := countShadowBranches()
   915→	gitHooksInstalled := strategy.IsGitHookInstalled()
   916→	claudeHooksInstalled := checkClaudeCodeHooksInstalled()
   917→	geminiHooksInstalled := checkGeminiCLIHooksInstalled()
   918→	entireDirExists := checkEntireDirExists()
   919→
   920→	// Check if there's anything to uninstall
   921→	if !entireDirExists && !gitHooksInstalled && sessionStateCount == 0 &&
   922→		shadowBranchCount == 0 && !claudeHooksInstalled && !geminiHooksInstalled {
   923→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "Entire is not installed in this repository.")
   924→		return nil
   925→	}
   926→
   927→	// Show confirmation prompt unless --force
   928→	if !force {
   929→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nThis will completely remove Entire from this repository:")
   930→		if entireDirExists {
   931→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - .entire/ directory")
   932→		}
   933→		if gitHooksInstalled {
   934→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - Git hooks (prepare-commit-msg, commit-msg, post-commit, pre-push)")
   935→		}
   936→		if sessionStateCount > 0 {
   937→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "  - Session state files (%d)\n", sessionStateCount)
   938→		}
   939→		if shadowBranchCount > 0 {
   940→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "  - Shadow branches (%d)\n", shadowBranchCount)
   941→		}
   942→		switch {
   943→		case claudeHooksInstalled && geminiHooksInstalled:
   944→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - Agent hooks (Claude Code, Gemini CLI)")
   945→		case claudeHooksInstalled:
   946→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - Agent hooks (Claude Code)")
   947→		case geminiHooksInstalled:
   948→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - Agent hooks (Gemini CLI)")
   949→		}
   950→		fmt.Fprintln(w)
   951→
   952→		var confirmed bool
   953→		form := NewAccessibleForm(
   954→			huh.NewGroup(
   955→				huh.NewConfirm().
   956→					Title("Are you sure you want to uninstall Entire?").
   957→					Affirmative("Yes, uninstall").
   958→					Negative("Cancel").
   959→					Value(&confirmed),
   960→			),
   961→		)
   962→
   963→		if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
   964→			return fmt.Errorf("confirmation cancelled: %w", err)
   965→		}
   966→
   967→		if !confirmed {
   968→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "Uninstall cancelled.")
   969→			return nil
   970→		}
   971→	}
   972→
   973→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nUninstalling Entire CLI...")
   974→
   975→	// 1. Remove agent hooks (lowest risk)
   976→	if err := removeAgentHooks(w); err != nil {
   977→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove agent hooks: %v\n", err)
   978→	}
   979→
   980→	// 2. Remove git hooks
   981→	removed, err := strategy.RemoveGitHook()
   982→	if err != nil {
   983→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove git hooks: %v\n", err)
   984→	} else if removed > 0 {
   985→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Removed git hooks (%d)\n", removed)
   986→	}
   987→
   988→	// 3. Remove session state files
   989→	statesRemoved, err := removeAllSessionStates()
   990→	if err != nil {
   991→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove session states: %v\n", err)
   992→	} else if statesRemoved > 0 {
   993→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Removed session states (%d)\n", statesRemoved)
   994→	}
   995→
   996→	// 4. Remove .entire/ directory
   997→	if err := removeEntireDirectory(); err != nil {
   998→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove .entire directory: %v\n", err)
   999→	} else if entireDirExists {
  1000→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Removed .entire directory")
  1001→	}
  1002→
  1003→	// 5. Remove shadow branches
  1004→	branchesRemoved, err := removeAllShadowBranches()
  1005→	if err != nil {
  1006→		fmt.Fprintf(errW, "Warning: failed to remove shadow branches: %v\n", err)
  1007→	} else if branchesRemoved > 0 {
  1008→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Removed %d shadow branches\n", branchesRemoved)
  1009→	}
  1010→
  1011→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nEntire CLI uninstalled successfully.")
  1012→	return nil
  1013→}
  1014→
  1015→// countSessionStates returns the number of active session state files.
  1016→func countSessionStates() int {
  1017→	store, err := session.NewStateStore()
  1018→	if err != nil {
  1019→		return 0
  1020→	}
  1021→	states, err := store.List(context.Background())
  1022→	if err != nil {
  1023→		return 0
  1024→	}
  1025→	return len(states)
  1026→}
  1027→
  1028→// countShadowBranches returns the number of shadow branches.
  1029→func countShadowBranches() int {
  1030→	branches, err := strategy.ListShadowBranches()
  1031→	if err != nil {
  1032→		return 0
  1033→	}
  1034→	return len(branches)
  1035→}
  1036→
  1037→// checkClaudeCodeHooksInstalled checks if Claude Code hooks are installed.
  1038→func checkClaudeCodeHooksInstalled() bool {
  1039→	ag, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentNameClaudeCode)
  1040→	if err != nil {
  1041→		return false
  1042→	}
  1043→	hookAgent, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport)
  1044→	if !ok {
  1045→		return false
  1046→	}
  1047→	return hookAgent.AreHooksInstalled()
  1048→}
  1049→
  1050→// checkGeminiCLIHooksInstalled checks if Gemini CLI hooks are installed.
  1051→func checkGeminiCLIHooksInstalled() bool {
  1052→	ag, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentNameGemini)
  1053→	if err != nil {
  1054→		return false
  1055→	}
  1056→	hookAgent, ok := ag.(agent.HookSupport)
  1057→	if !ok {
  1058→		return false
  1059→	}
  1060→	return hookAgent.AreHooksInstalled()
  1061→}
  1062→
  1063→// checkEntireDirExists checks if the .entire directory exists.
  1064→func checkEntireDirExists() bool {
  1065→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
  1066→	if err != nil {
  1067→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir
  1068→	}
  1069→	_, err = os.Stat(entireDirAbs)
  1070→	return err == nil
  1071→}
  1072→
  1073→// removeAgentHooks removes hooks from all agents that support hooks.
  1074→func removeAgentHooks(w io.Writer) error {
  1075→	var errs []error
  1076→
  1077→	// Remove Claude Code hooks
  1078→	claudeAgent, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentNameClaudeCode)
  1079→	if err == nil {
  1080→		if hookAgent, ok := claudeAgent.(agent.HookSupport); ok {
  1081→			wasInstalled := hookAgent.AreHooksInstalled()
  1082→			if err := hookAgent.UninstallHooks(); err != nil {
  1083→				errs = append(errs, err)
  1084→			} else if wasInstalled {
  1085→				fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Removed Claude Code hooks")
  1086→			}
  1087→		}
  1088→	}
  1089→
  1090→	// Remove Gemini CLI hooks
  1091→	geminiAgent, err := agent.Get(agent.AgentNameGemini)
  1092→	if err == nil {
  1093→		if hookAgent, ok := geminiAgent.(agent.HookSupport); ok {
  1094→			wasInstalled := hookAgent.AreHooksInstalled()
  1095→			if err := hookAgent.UninstallHooks(); err != nil {
  1096→				errs = append(errs, err)
  1097→			} else if wasInstalled {
  1098→				fmt.Fprintln(w, "  Removed Gemini CLI hooks")
  1099→			}
  1100→		}
  1101→	}
  1102→
  1103→	return errors.Join(errs...)
  1104→}
  1105→
  1106→// removeAllSessionStates removes all session state files and the directory.
  1107→func removeAllSessionStates() (int, error) {
  1108→	store, err := session.NewStateStore()
  1109→	if err != nil {
  1110→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to create state store: %w", err)
  1111→	}
  1112→
  1113→	// Count states before removing
  1114→	states, err := store.List(context.Background())
  1115→	if err != nil {
  1116→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to list session states: %w", err)
  1117→	}
  1118→	count := len(states)
  1119→
  1120→	// Remove the entire directory
  1121→	if err := store.RemoveAll(); err != nil {
  1122→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to remove session states: %w", err)
  1123→	}
  1124→
  1125→	return count, nil
  1126→}
  1127→
  1128→// removeEntireDirectory removes the .entire directory.
  1129→func removeEntireDirectory() error {
  1130→	entireDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(paths.EntireDir)
  1131→	if err != nil {
  1132→		entireDirAbs = paths.EntireDir
  1133→	}
  1134→	if err := os.RemoveAll(entireDirAbs); err != nil {
  1135→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove .entire directory: %w", err)
  1136→	}
  1137→	return nil
  1138→}
  1139→
  1140→// removeAllShadowBranches removes all shadow branches.
  1141→func removeAllShadowBranches() (int, error) {
  1142→	branches, err := strategy.ListShadowBranches()
  1143→	if err != nil {
  1144→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to list shadow branches: %w", err)
  1145→	}
  1146→	if len(branches) == 0 {
  1147→		return 0, nil
  1148→	}
  1149→	deleted, _, err := strategy.DeleteShadowBranches(branches)
  1150→	return len(deleted), err
  1151→}
  1152→
```

```json
  "new_string": "\t\tt.Run(tt.path, func(t *testing.T) {\n\t\t\tt.Parallel()\n\t\t\tif got := isProtectedPath(tt.path); got != tt.protected {\n\t\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"isProtectedPath(%q) = %v, want %v\", tt.path, got, tt.protected)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t})\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestIsEmptyRepository(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Run(\"empty repo returns true\", func(t *testing.T) {\n\t\tdir := t.TempDir()\n\t\trepo, err := git.PlainInit(dir, false)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to init repo: %v\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif !IsEmptyRepository(repo) {\n\t\t\tt.Error(\"IsEmptyRepository() = false, want true for empty repo\")\n\t\t}\n\t})\n\n\tt.Run(\"repo with commit returns false\", func(t *testing.T) {\n\t\tdir := t.TempDir()\n\t\trepo, err := git.PlainInit(dir, false)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to init repo: %v\", err)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Create a commit\n\t\ttestFile := filepath.Join(dir, \"test.txt\")\n\t\tif err := os.WriteFile(testFile, []byte(\"content\"), 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to write file: %v\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\twt, err := repo.Worktree()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to get worktree: %v\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif _, err := wt.Add(\"test.txt\"); err != nil {\n\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to add file: %v\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif _, err := wt.Commit(\"Initial commit\", &git.CommitOptions{\n\t\t\tAuthor: &object.Signature{Name: \"Test\", Email: \"test@test.com\"},\n\t\t}); err != nil {\n\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to commit: %v\", err)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif IsEmptyRepository(repo) {\n\t\t\tt.Error(\"IsEmptyRepository() = true, want false for repo with commit\")\n\t\t}\n\t})\n}"
}
```

All tests pass, including integration tests. Here's a summary of all changes made:

## Changes

### 1. `strategy/strategy.go` — Added `ErrEmptyRepository` sentinel error
New sentinel alongside existing errors, used by both strategies.

### 2. `strategy/common.go` — Added `IsEmptyRepository()` helper
Checks if `repo.Head()` returns `plumbing.ErrReferenceNotFound`, which indicates no commits exist.

### 3. `strategy/manual_commit_session.go` — Detect empty repo in `initializeSession`
Before the generic "failed to get HEAD" error, checks for the reference-not-found case and wraps `ErrEmptyRepository` with an actionable message. Added `"errors"` import.

### 4. `strategy/auto_commit.go` — Detect empty repo in `InitializeSession`
Same pattern as manual-commit.

### 5. `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` — Friendly message for empty repo
When `InitializeSession` returns `ErrEmptyRepository`, prints a user-friendly note instead of the generic warning. Added `"errors"` import.

### 6. `setup.go` — Warning during `entire enable`
After the git repo check, detects empty repos and prints a note that checkpoints won't work until the first commit.

### 7. Tests
- **`common_test.go`** — `TestIsEmptyRepository`: verifies true for empty repo, false for repo with commits
- **`manual_commit_test.go`** — `TestManualCommit_InitializeSession_EmptyRepo`: verifies `ErrEmptyRepository` is returned
- **`auto_commit_test.go`** — `TestAutoCommit_InitializeSession_EmptyRepo`: verifies `ErrEmptyRepository` is returned

```
+// TestAutoCommit_InitializeSession_EmptyRepo verifies that InitializeSession
+// returns ErrEmptyRepository when called on a repo with no commits.
+// Uses t.Chdir (process-global state), so cannot use t.Parallel().
+func TestAutoCommit_InitializeSession_EmptyRepo(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := t.TempDir()
+	_, err := git.PlainInit(dir, false)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("failed to init repo: %v", err)
+	}
+
+	t.Chdir(dir)
+
+	s := &AutoCommitStrategy{}
+	err = s.InitializeSession("test-session", agent.AgentTypeClaudeCode, "/tmp/transcript.jsonl", "hello")
+	if err == nil {
+		t.Fatal("InitializeSession() should return error for empty repo")
+	}
+	if !errors.Is(err, ErrEmptyRepository) {
+		t.Errorf("InitializeSession() error = %v, want ErrEmptyRepository", err)
+	}
+}
diff --git a/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/common.go b/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/common.go
index 211c4205..163a48d6 100644
--- a/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/common.go
+++ b/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/common.go
@@ -38,6 +38,14 @@ const (
 // Each package needs its own package-scoped sentinel for git log iteration patterns.
 var errStop = errors.New("stop iteration")
 
+// IsEmptyRepository returns true if the repository has no commits yet.
+// This is detected by checking if HEAD exists — a freshly git-init'd repo
+// has no HEAD reference until the first commit is created.
+func IsEmptyRepository(repo *git.Repository) bool {
+	_, err := repo.Head()
+	return errors.Is(err, plumbing.ErrReferenceNotFound)
+}
+
 // IsAncestorOf checks if commit is an ancestor of (or equal to) target.
 // Returns true if target can reach commit by following parent links.
 // Limits search to 1000 commits to avoid excessive traversal.

 		})

 }
+
+func TestIsEmptyRepository(t *testing.T) {
+	t.Run("empty repo returns true", func(t *testing.T) {
+		dir := t.TempDir()
+		repo, err := git.PlainInit(dir, false)
+		if err != nil {
+			t.Fatalf("failed to init repo: %v", err)
+		}
+		if !IsEmptyRepository(repo) {
+			t.Error("IsEmptyRepository() = false, want true for empty repo")
+		}
+	})
+
+	t.Run("repo with commit returns false", func(t *testing.T) {
+		dir := t.TempDir()
+		repo, err := git.PlainInit(dir, false)
+		if err != nil {
+			t.Fatalf("failed to init repo: %v", err)
+		}
+
+		// Create a commit
+		testFile := filepath.Join(dir, "test.txt")
+		if err := os.WriteFile(testFile, []byte("content"), 0o644); err != nil {
+			t.Fatalf("failed to write file: %v", err)
+		}
+		wt, err := repo.Worktree()
+		if err != nil {
+			t.Fatalf("failed to get worktree: %v", err)
+		}
+		if _, err := wt.Add("test.txt"); err != nil {
+			t.Fatalf("failed to add file: %v", err)
+		}
+		if _, err := wt.Commit("Initial commit", &git.CommitOptions{
+			Author: &object.Signature{Name: "Test", Email: "test@test.com"},
+		}); err != nil {
+			t.Fatalf("failed to commit: %v", err)
+		}
+
+		if IsEmptyRepository(repo) {
+			t.Error("IsEmptyRepository() = true, want false for repo with commit")
+		}
+	})
+}
```
