> DEVELOPER

in setup.go we now call strategy.OpenRepository(). do we need to close the repositiory again?

> AGENT

No. go-git's `git.PlainOpenWithOptions()` returns a `*git.Repository` that has no `Close()` method — it's not a resource that needs cleanup. It just wraps access to the `.git` directory on disk. So the current code is fine.

> DEVELOPER

rather than modifying intiializesession for manual_commit_session.go and auto_commit.go, could we call 'IsEmptyRepository' inside common.go:OpenRepository()?

> AGENT

That would be risky. `OpenRepository()` is called broadly across the codebase — `ValidateRepository`, `EnsureSetup`, `ListCheckpoints`, `checkCanRewind`, `GetMetadataBranchTree`, etc. Most of those callers don't need HEAD and work fine on empty repos. Returning an error from `OpenRepository()` for empty repos would break them.

For example, `entire enable` itself calls `strategy.OpenRepository()` (both in our new warning check and later via `strat.EnsureSetup()`). If `OpenRepository` rejected empty repos, `entire enable` would fail instead of just warning.

The current approach keeps the check scoped to the two places that actually need HEAD — session initialization — which is where the original bug manifests.

     1→// hooks_geminicli_handlers.go contains Gemini CLI specific hook handler implementations.
     2→// These are called by the hook registry in hook_registry.go.
     3→package cli
     4→
     5→import (
     6→	"context"
     7→	"errors"
     8→	"fmt"
     9→	"log/slog"
    10→	"os"
    11→	"path/filepath"
    12→	"strings"
    13→
    14→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    15→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli"
    16→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging"
    17→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    18→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy"
    19→)
    20→
    21→// ErrSessionSkipped is returned when a session should be skipped (e.g., due to concurrent warning).
    22→var ErrSessionSkipped = errors.New("session skipped")
    23→
    24→// handleGeminiSessionStart handles the SessionStart hook for Gemini CLI.
    25→func handleGeminiSessionStart() error {
    26→	return handleSessionStartCommon()
    27→}
    28→
    29→// handleGeminiSessionEnd handles the SessionEnd hook for Gemini CLI.
    30→// This fires when the user explicitly exits the session (via "exit" or "logout" commands).
    31→// It parses the session-end input and marks the session as ended so that subsequent
    32→// git hooks (e.g., post-commit) can trigger condensation for ended sessions.
    33→func handleGeminiSessionEnd() error { // Parse stdin once upfront — all subsequent steps use ctx.sessionID
    34→	ctx, err := parseGeminiSessionEnd()
    35→	if err != nil {
    36→		if errors.Is(err, ErrSessionSkipped) {
    37→			return nil
    38→		}
    39→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse session-end input: %w", err)
    40→	}
    41→
    42→	// Mark session as ended using the parsed session ID
    43→	if err := markSessionEnded(ctx.sessionID); err != nil {
    44→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to mark session ended: %v\n", err)
    45→	}
    46→
    47→	return nil
    48→}
    49→
    50→// geminiSessionContext holds parsed session data for Gemini commits.
    51→type geminiSessionContext struct {
    52→	sessionID      string
    53→	transcriptPath string
    54→	sessionDir     string
    55→	sessionDirAbs  string
    56→	transcriptData []byte
    57→	allPrompts     []string
    58→	summary        string
    59→	modifiedFiles  []string
    60→	commitMessage  string
    61→}
    62→
    63→// parseGeminiSessionEnd parses the session-end hook input and validates transcript.
    64→func parseGeminiSessionEnd() (*geminiSessionContext, error) {
    65→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
    66→	if err != nil {
    67→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
    68→	}
    69→
    70→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookSessionEnd, os.Stdin)
    71→	if err != nil {
    72→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
    73→	}
    74→
    75→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
    76→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-end",
    77→		slog.String("hook", "session-end"),
    78→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
    79→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
    80→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
    81→	)
    82→
    83→	sessionID := input.SessionID
    84→	if sessionID == "" {
    85→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
    86→	}
    87→
    88→	transcriptPath := input.SessionRef
    89→	if transcriptPath == "" || !fileExists(transcriptPath) {
    90→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("transcript file not found or empty: %s", transcriptPath)
    91→	}
    92→
    93→	return &geminiSessionContext{
    94→		sessionID:      sessionID,
    95→		transcriptPath: transcriptPath,
    96→	}, nil
    97→}
    98→
    99→// setupGeminiSessionDir creates session directory and copies transcript.
   100→func setupGeminiSessionDir(ctx *geminiSessionContext) error {
   101→	ctx.sessionDir = paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(ctx.sessionID)
   102→	sessionDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(ctx.sessionDir)
   103→	if err != nil {
   104→		sessionDirAbs = ctx.sessionDir
   105→	}
   106→	ctx.sessionDirAbs = sessionDirAbs
   107→
   108→	if err := os.MkdirAll(sessionDirAbs, 0o750); err != nil {
   109→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session directory: %w", err)
   110→	}
   111→
   112→	logFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName)
   113→	if err := copyFile(ctx.transcriptPath, logFile); err != nil {
   114→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to copy transcript: %w", err)
   115→	}
   116→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Copied transcript to: %s\n", ctx.sessionDir+"/"+paths.TranscriptFileName)
   117→
   118→	transcriptData, err := os.ReadFile(ctx.transcriptPath)
   119→	if err != nil {
   120→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   121→	}
   122→	ctx.transcriptData = transcriptData
   123→
   124→	return nil
   125→}
   126→
   127→// extractGeminiMetadata extracts prompts, summary, and modified files from transcript.
   128→func extractGeminiMetadata(ctx *geminiSessionContext) error {
   129→	allPrompts, err := geminicli.ExtractAllUserPrompts(ctx.transcriptData)
   130→	if err != nil {
   131→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract prompts: %v\n", err)
   132→	}
   133→	ctx.allPrompts = allPrompts
   134→
   135→	promptFile := filepath.Join(ctx.sessionDirAbs, paths.PromptFileName)
   136→	promptContent := strings.Join(allPrompts, "\n\n---\n\n")
   137→	if err := os.WriteFile(promptFile, []byte(promptContent), 0o600); err != nil {
   138→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write prompt file: %w", err)
   139→	}
   140→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted %d prompt(s) to: %s\n", len(allPrompts), ctx.sessionDir+"/"+paths.PromptFileName)
   141→
   142→	summary, err := geminicli.ExtractLastAssistantMessage(ctx.transcriptData)
   143→	if err != nil {
   144→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract summary: %v\n", err)
   145→	}
   146→	ctx.summary = summary
   147→
   148→	summaryFile := filepath.Join(ctx.sessionDirAbs, paths.SummaryFileName)
   149→	if err := os.WriteFile(summaryFile, []byte(summary), 0o600); err != nil {
   150→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write summary file: %w", err)
   151→	}
   152→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted summary to: %s\n", ctx.sessionDir+"/"+paths.SummaryFileName)
   153→
   154→	modifiedFiles, err := geminicli.ExtractModifiedFiles(ctx.transcriptData)
   155→	if err != nil {
   156→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract modified files: %v\n", err)
   157→	}
   158→	ctx.modifiedFiles = modifiedFiles
   159→
   160→	lastPrompt := ""
   161→	if len(allPrompts) > 0 {
   162→		lastPrompt = allPrompts[len(allPrompts)-1]
   163→	}
   164→	ctx.commitMessage = generateCommitMessage(lastPrompt)
   165→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Using commit message: %s\n", ctx.commitMessage)
   166→
   167→	return nil
   168→}
   169→
   170→// commitGeminiSession commits the session changes using the strategy.
   171→func commitGeminiSession(ctx *geminiSessionContext) error {
   172→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   173→	if err != nil {
   174→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   175→	}
   176→
   177→	preState, err := LoadPrePromptState(ctx.sessionID)
   178→	if err != nil {
   179→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   180→	}
   181→	if preState != nil {
   182→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Loaded pre-prompt state: %d pre-existing untracked files, start message index: %d\n", len(preState.UntrackedFiles), preState.StartMessageIndex)
   183→	}
   184→
   185→	// Get transcript position from pre-prompt state
   186→	var startMessageIndex int
   187→	if preState != nil {
   188→		startMessageIndex = preState.StartMessageIndex
   189→	}
   190→
   191→	// Calculate token usage for this prompt/response cycle (Gemini-specific)
   192→	var tokenUsage *agent.TokenUsage
   193→	if ctx.transcriptPath != "" {
   194→		usage, tokenErr := geminicli.CalculateTokenUsageFromFile(ctx.transcriptPath, startMessageIndex)
   195→		if tokenErr != nil {
   196→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to calculate token usage: %v\n", tokenErr)
   197→		} else if usage != nil && usage.APICallCount > 0 {
   198→			tokenUsage = usage
   199→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Token usage for this checkpoint: input=%d, output=%d, cache_read=%d, api_calls=%d\n",
   200→				tokenUsage.InputTokens, tokenUsage.OutputTokens, tokenUsage.CacheReadTokens, tokenUsage.APICallCount)
   201→		}
   202→	}
   203→
   204→	// Compute new and deleted files (single git status call)
   205→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preState.PreUntrackedFiles())
   206→	if err != nil {
   207→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   208→	}
   209→
   210→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(ctx.modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   211→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   212→	if changes != nil {
   213→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   214→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   215→	}
   216→
   217→	totalChanges := len(relModifiedFiles) + len(relNewFiles) + len(relDeletedFiles)
   218→	if totalChanges == 0 {
   219→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No files were modified during this session\n")
   220→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Skipping commit\n")
   221→		if cleanupErr := CleanupPrePromptState(ctx.sessionID); cleanupErr != nil {
   222→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", cleanupErr)
   223→		}
   224→		return nil
   225→	}
   226→
   227→	logFileChanges(relModifiedFiles, relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles)
   228→
   229→	contextFile := filepath.Join(ctx.sessionDirAbs, paths.ContextFileName)
   230→	if err := createContextFileForGemini(contextFile, ctx.commitMessage, ctx.sessionID, ctx.allPrompts, ctx.summary); err != nil {
   231→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create context file: %w", err)
   232→	}
   233→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Created context file: %s\n", ctx.sessionDir+"/"+paths.ContextFileName)
   234→
   235→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   236→	if err != nil {
   237→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   238→	}
   239→
   240→	strat := GetStrategy()
   241→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   242→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
   243→	}
   244→
   245→	// Get agent type from the hook agent (determined by which hook command is running)
   246→	// This is authoritative - if we're in "entire hooks gemini session-end", it's Gemini CLI
   247→	hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   248→	if agentErr != nil {
   249→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", agentErr)
   250→	}
   251→	agentType := hookAgent.Type()
   252→
   253→	// Get transcript identifier at start from pre-prompt state
   254→	var transcriptIdentifierAtStart string
   255→	if preState != nil {
   256→		transcriptIdentifierAtStart = preState.LastTranscriptIdentifier
   257→	}
   258→
   259→	saveCtx := strategy.SaveContext{
   260→		SessionID:                ctx.sessionID,
   261→		ModifiedFiles:            relModifiedFiles,
   262→		NewFiles:                 relNewFiles,
   263→		DeletedFiles:             relDeletedFiles,
   264→		MetadataDir:              ctx.sessionDir,
   265→		MetadataDirAbs:           ctx.sessionDirAbs,
   266→		CommitMessage:            ctx.commitMessage,
   267→		TranscriptPath:           ctx.transcriptPath,
   268→		AuthorName:               author.Name,
   269→		AuthorEmail:              author.Email,
   270→		AgentType:                agentType,
   271→		StepTranscriptStart:      startMessageIndex,
   272→		StepTranscriptIdentifier: transcriptIdentifierAtStart,
   273→		TokenUsage:               tokenUsage,
   274→	}
   275→
   276→	if err := strat.SaveChanges(saveCtx); err != nil {
   277→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session: %w", err)
   278→	}
   279→
   280→	if cleanupErr := CleanupPrePromptState(ctx.sessionID); cleanupErr != nil {
   281→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", cleanupErr)
   282→	}
   283→
   284→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Session saved successfully\n")
   285→	return nil
   286→}
   287→
   288→// logFileChanges logs the modified, new, and deleted files to stderr.
   289→func logFileChanges(modified, newFiles, deleted []string) {
   290→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files modified during session (%d):\n", len(modified))
   291→	for _, file := range modified {
   292→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   293→	}
   294→	if len(newFiles) > 0 {
   295→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "New files created (%d):\n", len(newFiles))
   296→		for _, file := range newFiles {
   297→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   298→		}
   299→	}
   300→	if len(deleted) > 0 {
   301→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files deleted (%d):\n", len(deleted))
   302→		for _, file := range deleted {
   303→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   304→		}
   305→	}
   306→}
   307→
   308→// createContextFileForGemini creates a context.md file for Gemini sessions.
   309→func createContextFileForGemini(contextFile, commitMessage, sessionID string, prompts []string, summary string) error {
   310→	var sb strings.Builder
   311→
   312→	sb.WriteString("# Session Context\n\n")
   313→	sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("Session ID: %s\n", sessionID))
   314→	sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("Commit Message: %s\n\n", commitMessage))
   315→
   316→	if len(prompts) > 0 {
   317→		sb.WriteString("## Prompts\n\n")
   318→		for i, p := range prompts {
   319→			sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("### Prompt %d\n\n%s\n\n", i+1, p))
   320→		}
   321→	}
   322→
   323→	if summary != "" {
   324→		sb.WriteString("## Summary\n\n")
   325→		sb.WriteString(summary)
   326→		sb.WriteString("\n")
   327→	}
   328→
   329→	if err := os.WriteFile(contextFile, []byte(sb.String()), 0o600); err != nil {
   330→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write context file: %w", err)
   331→	}
   332→	return nil
   333→}
   334→
   335→// handleGeminiBeforeTool handles the BeforeTool hook for Gemini CLI.
   336→// This is similar to Claude Code's PreToolUse hook but applies to all tools.
   337→func handleGeminiBeforeTool() error {
   338→	// Get the agent for hook input parsing
   339→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   340→	if err != nil {
   341→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   342→	}
   343→
   344→	// Parse hook input
   345→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookPreToolUse, os.Stdin)
   346→	if err != nil {
   347→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   348→	}
   349→
   350→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   351→	logging.Debug(logCtx, "gemini-before-tool",
   352→		slog.String("hook", "before-tool"),
   353→		slog.String("hook_type", "tool"),
   354→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   355→		slog.String("tool_name", input.ToolName),
   356→	)
   357→
   358→	// For now, BeforeTool is mainly for logging and potential future use
   359→	// We don't need to do anything special before tool execution
   360→	return nil
   361→}
   362→
   363→// handleGeminiAfterTool handles the AfterTool hook for Gemini CLI.
   364→// This is similar to Claude Code's PostToolUse hook but applies to all tools.
   365→func handleGeminiAfterTool() error {
   366→	// Get the agent for hook input parsing
   367→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   368→	if err != nil {
   369→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   370→	}
   371→
   372→	// Parse hook input
   373→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookPostToolUse, os.Stdin)
   374→	if err != nil {
   375→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   376→	}
   377→
   378→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   379→	logging.Debug(logCtx, "gemini-after-tool",
   380→		slog.String("hook", "after-tool"),
   381→		slog.String("hook_type", "tool"),
   382→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   383→		slog.String("tool_name", input.ToolName),
   384→	)
   385→
   386→	// For now, AfterTool is mainly for logging
   387→	// Future: Could be used for incremental checkpoints similar to Claude's PostTodo
   388→	return nil
   389→}
   390→
   391→// handleGeminiBeforeAgent handles the BeforeAgent hook for Gemini CLI.
   392→// This is equivalent to Claude Code's UserPromptSubmit - it fires when the user submits a prompt.
   393→// We capture the initial state here so we can track what files were modified during the session.
   394→// It also checks for concurrent sessions and blocks if another session has uncommitted changes.
   395→func handleGeminiBeforeAgent() error {
   396→	// Always use the Gemini agent for Gemini hooks (don't use GetAgent() which may
   397→	// return Claude based on auto-detection in environments like VSCode)
   398→	ag, err := agent.Get("gemini")
   399→	if err != nil {
   400→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get gemini agent: %w", err)
   401→	}
   402→
   403→	// Parse hook input - BeforeAgent provides user prompt info similar to UserPromptSubmit
   404→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookUserPromptSubmit, os.Stdin)
   405→	if err != nil {
   406→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   407→	}
   408→
   409→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   410→
   411→	// Log with prompt if available (Gemini provides the user's prompt in BeforeAgent)
   412→	logArgs := []any{
   413→		slog.String("hook", "before-agent"),
   414→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
   415→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   416→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
   417→	}
   418→	if input.UserPrompt != "" {
   419→		// Truncate long prompts for logging
   420→		promptPreview := input.UserPrompt
   421→		if len(promptPreview) > 100 {
   422→			promptPreview = promptPreview[:100] + "..."
   423→		}
   424→		logArgs = append(logArgs, slog.String("prompt_preview", promptPreview))
   425→	}
   426→	logging.Info(logCtx, "gemini-before-agent", logArgs...)
   427→
   428→	if input.SessionID == "" {
   429→		return errors.New("no session_id in input")
   430→	}
   431→
   432→	// Capture pre-prompt state with transcript position (Gemini-specific)
   433→	// This captures both untracked files and the current transcript message count
   434→	// so we can calculate token usage for just this prompt/response cycle
   435→	if err := CaptureGeminiPrePromptState(input.SessionID, input.SessionRef); err != nil {
   436→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to capture pre-prompt state: %w", err)
   437→	}
   438→
   439→	// If strategy implements SessionInitializer, call it to initialize session state
   440→	strat := GetStrategy()
   441→	if initializer, ok := strat.(strategy.SessionInitializer); ok {
   442→		agentType := ag.Type()
   443→		if err := initializer.InitializeSession(input.SessionID, agentType, input.SessionRef, input.UserPrompt); err != nil {
   444→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to initialize session state: %v\n", err)
   445→		}
   446→	}
   447→
   448→	return nil
   449→}
   450→
   451→// handleGeminiAfterAgent handles the AfterAgent hook for Gemini CLI.
   452→// This fires after the agent has finished processing and generated a response.
   453→// This is equivalent to Claude Code's "Stop" hook - it commits the session changes with metadata.
   454→// AfterAgent fires after EVERY user prompt/response cycle, making it the correct place
   455→// for checkpoint creation (not SessionEnd, which only fires on explicit exit).
   456→func handleGeminiAfterAgent() error {
   457→	// Always use Gemini agent for Gemini hooks
   458→	ag, err := agent.Get("gemini")
   459→	if err != nil {
   460→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get gemini agent: %w", err)
   461→	}
   462→
   463→	// Parse hook input using HookStop - AfterAgent provides the same data as Stop
   464→	// (session_id, transcript_path) which is what we need for committing
   465→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookStop, os.Stdin)
   466→	if err != nil {
   467→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   468→	}
   469→
   470→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   471→	logging.Info(logCtx, "gemini-after-agent",
   472→		slog.String("hook", "after-agent"),
   473→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
   474→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   475→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
   476→	)
   477→
   478→	sessionID := input.SessionID
   479→	if sessionID == "" {
   480→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
   481→	}
   482→
   483→	transcriptPath := input.SessionRef
   484→	if transcriptPath == "" || !fileExists(transcriptPath) {
   485→		return fmt.Errorf("transcript file not found or empty: %s", transcriptPath)
   486→	}
   487→
   488→	// Create session context and commit
   489→	ctx := &geminiSessionContext{
   490→		sessionID:      sessionID,
   491→		transcriptPath: transcriptPath,
   492→	}
   493→
   494→	if err := setupGeminiSessionDir(ctx); err != nil {
   495→		return err
   496→	}
   497→
   498→	if err := extractGeminiMetadata(ctx); err != nil {
   499→		return err
   500→	}
   501→
   502→	if err := commitGeminiSession(ctx); err != nil {
   503→		return err
   504→	}
   505→
   506→	// Transition session ACTIVE → IDLE (equivalent to Claude's transitionSessionTurnEnd)
   507→	transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   508→
   509→	return nil
   510→}
   511→
   512→// handleGeminiBeforeModel handles the BeforeModel hook for Gemini CLI.
   513→// This fires before every LLM call (potentially multiple times per agent loop).
   514→// Useful for logging/monitoring LLM requests.
   515→func handleGeminiBeforeModel() error {
   516→	// Get the agent for hook input parsing
   517→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   518→	if err != nil {
   519→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   520→	}
   521→
   522→	// Parse hook input - use HookPreToolUse as a generic hook type for now
   523→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookPreToolUse, os.Stdin)
   524→	if err != nil {
   525→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   526→	}
   527→
   528→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   529→	logging.Debug(logCtx, "gemini-before-model",
   530→		slog.String("hook", "before-model"),
   531→		slog.String("hook_type", "model"),
   532→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   533→	)
   534→
   535→	// For now, BeforeModel is mainly for logging
   536→	// Future: Could be used for request interception/modification
   537→	return nil
   538→}
